Outputs findings with pilot-dotnet entity-keys standard IDs. - **dotnet-error-handling**: Reviews ASP.NET Core error-handling architecture. Flags missing centralized exception-handling middleware (IExceptionHandler), error responses that don't follow the RFC 7807 ProblemDetails shape, exception detail (stack traces, messages) leaked to clients in production, and business/domain-rule failures thrown as generic exceptions instead of typed domain exceptions mapped to specific ProblemDetails types. Outputs findings with pilot-dotnet error-handling standard IDs. ([AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot](https://github.com/AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot/tree/HEAD/plugins/pilot-dotnet/skills/dotnet-error-handling) · ★2)
